# 字卡：0001670254-25-000685.txt.gz

## 摘要
這是 HEVO Inc. 於 2025-07-07 提交的 SEC Form C 完整 SGML 封存，含 16 個 DOCUMENT 及 14 份 PDF 共 172 頁，申報簽署欄日期為 07-03-2025。
本次完整串流解析封存並核對全部附件，14 份 PDF 與獨立附件 SHA-256 完全相同，XML／HTML 正文經明確包裝正規化後亦相同；這些內容均屬同源材料。
最重要的增量是補回 SEC 標頭、完整附件結構與封存定位，並沿用全部附件已校正字卡；既有財務與法律疑點及未完成目視覆蓋仍須保留。

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.22 | We have audited the consolidated financial statements of HEVO Inc., which comprise the consolidated balance | p.82；附件原 p.3 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.25 | statements. In our opinion, the accompanying consolidated financial statements present fairly, in all material | p.82；附件原 p.3 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.31 | Basis for Opinion | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.33 | We conducted our audits in accordance with auditing standards generally accepted in the United States of America | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.34 | (GAAS). Our responsibilities under those standards are further described in the Auditor's Responsibilities for the | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.35 | Audit of the Consolidated Financial Statements section of our report. We are required to be independent of HEVO | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.36 | Inc. and to meet our other ethical responsibilities, in accordance with the relevant ethical requirements relating to | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.37 | our audits. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.38 | our audit opinion. | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.40 | Responsibilities of Management for the Consolidated Financial Statements | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.42 | Management is responsible for the preparation and fair presentation of the consolidated financial statements in | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.43 | accordance with accounting principles generally accepted in the United States of America, and for the design, | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.44 | implementation, and maintenance of internal control relevant to the preparation and fair presentation of | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.45 | consolidated financial statements that are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error. In | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.46 | preparing the consolidated financial statements, management is required to evaluate whether there are conditions | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.47 | or events, considered in the aggregate, that raise substantial doubt about HEVO Inc.’s ability to continue as a going | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.49 | concern. | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.52 | Auditor’s Responsibilities for the Audit of the Consolidated Financial Statements | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.55 | Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.56 | are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor's report that includes | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.57 | our opinion. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance but is not absolute assurance and therefore is not a | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.58 | guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with GAAS will always detect a material misstatement when it | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.59 | exists. The risk of not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.60 | error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of internal | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.61 | control. Misstatements are considered material if there is a substantial likelihood that, individually or in the | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.62 | aggregate, they would influence the judgment made by a reasonable user based on the consolidated financial | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.64 | statements. | p.82；附件原 p.3 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 3.67 | * | p.82；附件原 p.3 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.6 | In performing an audit in accordance with GAAS, we: | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.8 | e Exercise professional judgment and maintain professional skepticism throughout the audit. | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.9 | e Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the consolidated financial statements, whether | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.10 | due to fraud or error, and design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks. Such procedures | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.11 | include examining, on a test basis, evidence regarding the amounts and disclosures in the consolidated | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.12 | financial statements. | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.13 | ¢ Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit in order to design audit procedures that | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.14 | are appropriate in the circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing an opinion on the effectiveness | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.15 | of HEVO Inc.’s internal control. Accordingly, no such opinion is expressed. | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.16 | e Evaluate the appropriateness of accounting policies used and the reasonableness of significant accounting | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.17 | estimates made by management, as well as evaluate the overall presentation of the consolidated financial | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.19 | statements. | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.21 | e Conclude whether, in our judgment, there are conditions or events, considered in the aggregate, that raise | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.22 | substantial doubt about HEVO Inc.’s ability to continue as a going concern for a reasonable period of time. | p.83；附件原 p.4 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 4.25 | We are required to communicate with those charged with governance regarding, among other matters, the planned | p.83；附件原 p.4 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.8 | NOTE 1— NATURE OF OPERATIONS AND CONSOLIDATION | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.11 | HEVO, Inc. was organized under the laws of the State of Delaware on November 1, 2011. The | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.12 | Company was founded to provide safe, seamless, and universal wireless charging equipment and | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.13 | software that can improve the efficiency of charging electric vehicles (EV). The Company’s | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.14 | product, the Rezonant E8 is an 8kW Level 2 charger is the “only wireless and plug-in capable electric | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.15 | vehicle charger in the world”. The Rezonant E8 is the “first wireless charger that has met SAE-J2954 | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.16 | specifications and has also completed UL-2750 safety testing”. The Company’s headquarters are in | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.17 | Brooklyn, New York. | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.20 | Since November 1, 2011, the Company has relied upon its shareholders and grants for funding cash | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.21 | flow to pay for operating expenses (see discussions below). For the period from inception to | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.22 | December 31, 2024, the Company has generated losses aggregating more than $23 million. These | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.23 | matters do raise concern about the Company’s ability to continue as a going concern (see Note 14). | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.26 | Principles of Consolidation | p.89；附件原 p.10 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.29 | subsidiaries HEVO Europe BV and HEVO Power Limited (collectively, which may be referred to as | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.30 | the “Company”, “we,” “us,” or “our”). All intercompany transactions and balances have been | p.89；附件原 p.10 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.31 | eliminated in consolidation. The results of operations for the years ended December 31, 2024 and | p.89；附件原 p.10 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 10.33 | respective dates of incorporation. | p.89；附件原 p.10 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 13.48 | observable for the asset or liability, either directly or indirectly, including quoted prices for | p.92；附件原 p.13 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.11 | lease, or if not readily determinable, the Company’s incremental borrowing rate. | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.13 | ROU assets are initially measured based on the lease liability, adjusted for any lease payments made | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.14 | at or before the commencement date, lease incentives received, and initial direct costs incurred. | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.15 | The ROU asset is subsequently amortized over the lease term on a straight-line basis. Lease | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.16 | liabilities are increased to reflect interest on the liability and reduced to reflect lease payments | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.17 | made during the period. | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.19 | Operating lease expense is recognized on a straight-line basis over the lease term within the | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.20 | Company’s operating expenses. The Company evaluates its lease agreements to determine | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.21 | whether a contract conveys the right to control the use of an identified asset and whether it | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.22 | qualifies as an operating or finance lease. Short-term leases with an initial term of 12 months or less | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.23 | are not recorded on the balance sheet, and lease expense is recognized as incurred. | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.26 | Accounts Payable | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.28 | Accounts payable are recorded at their invoiced amounts and represent obligations to pay for | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.29 | goods or services received. They are classified as current liabilities unless payment is not due within | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.30 | one year. Accounts payable are recognized at the transaction date and are subsequently measured | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.31 | at amortized cost, which typically equals the original invoiced amount. | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.33 | Accrued Expenses | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.35 | Accrued expenses represent liabilities for goods or services that have been received but not yet | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.36 | paid for as of the reporting date. These include accrued compensation and payroll liabilities, | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.37 | royalties, interest on outstanding debt, and other routine operating expenses. These expenses are | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.38 | recognized when incurred, based on the best estimate of the amount owed, even if no formal invoice | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.39 | has been received. Accrued expenses are classified as current liabilities and are typically settled | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.40 | within the normal operating cycle. | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.42 | Stock and Equity Based Compensation | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.44 | Consistent with US GAAP, the Company records stock-based compensation as a non-cash | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.46 | expense. | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.48 | The Company measures and recognizes compensation expenses for all stock-based awards, | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.49 | granted to employees and directors based on the estimated fair value of the awards on the | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.50 | date of grant. The fair value of each stock option award is estimated on the grant date using | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.51 | the Black-Scholes option pricing model. The Black-Scholes option pricing model requires the | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.52 | input of highly subjective assumptions, including the fair value of the underlying common stock, | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.53 | the expected term of the option, the expected volatility of the price of the Company’s common | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.54 | stock, risk-free interest rates, and the expected dividend yield of the Company’s common | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.55 | stock. The assumptions used to determine the fair value of the awards represent | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.56 | management’s best estimates. These estimates involve inherent uncertainties and the | p.93；附件原 p.14 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 14.57 | application of management’s judgment. | p.93；附件原 p.14 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.13 | Revenue Recognition | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.16 | The Company recognizes revenue primarily from the following types of contracts: | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.18 | e Product sales - Revenue is recognized at the point where the customer obtains control of | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.19 | the goods and the Company satisfies its performance obligation, which generally is at the | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.20 | time it ships the product to the customer. | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.21 | e Services - Consists of revenues from provision of engineering, installation and training | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.22 | services. Revenue from these services is recognized as the services are rendered. | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.23 | e Shipping income. | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.26 | Shipping and handling activities performed by the Company before the transfer of control of a good | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.27 | are fulfillment activities rather than promised services and are included in cost of goods. For | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.28 | shipping and handling activities performed after the customer obtains controls of the good, as | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.29 | permitted under ASC 606-10-25-18B the company has elected to account for shipping and handling | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.30 | as a promised service to the customer. As a result, any amounts received from customers for such | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.31 | activities are recognized as revenue and the costs of providing the service are recorded in cost of | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.32 | goods sold. | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.35 | The timing of the Company’s revenue recognition may differ from the timing of payment by its | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.36 | customers. A receivable is recorded when revenue is recognized prior to payment and the Company | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.37 | has an unconditional right to payment. Alternatively, when payment precedes the provision of | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.38 | goods or services, the Company records deferred revenue until the performance obligations are | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.39 | satisfied. As of December 31, 2024 and 2023, the Company has deferred revenue amounting to $O | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.40 | and $29,050, respectively. | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.42 | In 2024 and 2023, revenue recognized from deferred revenue performance obligations generated in | p.94；附件原 p.15 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 15.43 | previous periods totaled $0 and $87,670, respectively. | p.94；附件原 p.15 |

| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.19 | Income Taxes | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.22 | The Company accounts for income taxes under the asset and liability method, as required by ASC | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.23 | 740, Income Taxes. Under this method, deferred income tax assets and liabilities are recognized for | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.24 | the expected future tax consequences of temporary differences between the financial reporting | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.25 | and tax bases of assets and liabilities, and for net operating loss (“NOL”) and tax credit | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.26 | carryforwards. Income taxes are provided for the tax effects of transactions reporting in the | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.27 | consolidated financial statements and consist of currently due taxes plus deferred taxes related | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.28 | primarily to differences between the basis of receivables, inventory, property and equipment, | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.29 | intangible assets, and accrued expenses for financial and income tax reporting. The deferred tax | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.30 | assets and liabilities represent the future tax return consequences of those differences, which will | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.31 | either be taxable or deductible when the assets and liabilities are recovered or settled. Deferred tax | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.32 | assets are reduced by a valuation allowance when, in the opinion of management, it is more likely | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.33 | than not that some portion or all of the deferred tax assets will not be realized. | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.36 | As of December 31, 2024 and 2023, the Company had no provision for federal or state income taxes | p.95；附件原 p.16 |
| document_5.pdf／原文逐行／條款 16.37 | due to operating losses incurred since inception. The Company has not recognized any deferred | p.95；附件原 p.16 |

## 未明名詞與疑點
- p.1：SEC 標頭 business address 為 147 PRINCE ST., BROOKLYN NY 11201；mail address 與 XML issuer address 為 117 8TH ST, BROOKLYN NY 11215-3115。不同地址用途已標示，尚不能當成自相矛盾；需確認現行登記／營業據點。
- p.1：former company HEVO Inc 與 HEVO Inc. 僅標點不同；分別記錄 20210127、20130920 名稱變更日，不能據此認定不同法人。CIK 0001587317 是本發行人；0001670254 是申報 portal 的 CIK，不能混作 HEVO 的 CIK。
- p.2：XML MostRecentFiscalYear／PriorFiscalYear 財務欄未直接列年度；不得以 2025 申報日期推定財務期間，也不得將 shortTermDebt 等同全部有息借款。
- p.2：XML offeringAmount 50000.00、maximumOfferingAmount 5000000.00、noOfSecurityOffered 25012、price 1.99910 是不同募集口徑；不能把目標股數當成實際已發行股份或募資上限股數。
- p.2：XML 7.9%（0.079）成功募資報酬之外另有第三方支出報銷；總成本不能僅以 7.9% 推估。overSubscriptionAllocationType=Other、As determined by the issuer 未列更精細分配規則。
- p.2：XML issuerWebsite／coIssuerWebsite 是欄位值；本次未連線驗證網站、公司現況或截至 04-30-2026 的募集結果。

## 覆蓋聲明
本封存沒有原生實體頁碼。本次建立共 176 個邏輯定位頁 p.1–p.176：SEC 標頭／附件清單 1 頁、XML 2 頁、14 份 PDF 172 頁、HTML 1 頁。SGML 共 3,271,522 行已完整串流處理；16 個 DOCUMENT 均解析且與獨立附件核對；XML 全部 133 個 leaf（非空 130、空白 3）逐項列入。關鍵數字表 6120 列為機械抽取／轉錄列，並非 6120 個互相獨立事實。

14 份 PDF 共 172 頁均無原生文字，全部以已存在的逐頁 OCR 字卡轉錄重用，包含既有目視更正；本次未重新做 OCR，也未新增目視讀頁。既有字卡記錄 33 頁視覺核對，餘 139 頁沒有既有目視核對紀錄；這是明確未覆蓋範圍，不能稱已完成全頁視覺驗收。掃描頁 `needs_ocr=true` 與 `ocr_text_available=true` 同時保留，避免將 OCR 字串當成原生 PDF 文字。document_1.pdf p.24 與 document_2.pdf p.28 沿用既有空白頁目視紀錄；document_2.pdf p.11 為照片頁，OCR 幾乎無文字，未將其當作空白頁。
